AV Profile evidence report

Reproduction details

External reproduction is UNPERFORMED. An agent-local checkout is local verification and cannot establish independent reproduction.

Follow docs/profiles/AV-REPORT-REPRODUCTION.md at the recorded implementation commit. A later artifact commit may contain this evidence without changing the evidence commit.

  1. Build and execute an independent candidate at a recorded source revision using the adapter contract in tests/README.md.
  2. Have an independent human or organization regenerate the report from a clean checkout using docs/profiles/AV-REPORT-REPRODUCTION.md.
  3. Record elapsed time, host, exact commands, transcript and both artifacts; review differences and publish the evidence at an operator-selected location.
